## What's Done (recent)

Iterations 32–38 addressed the pain-ranked issues the user flagged as "болит каждый день":

- **Inline edit + trim history** on any user message (Pencil), plus retry-as-rerun (RotateCcw) that also trims. Backend `EditUserMessage(projectID, sessionID, indexFromEnd, newText)` holds session lock across active-check and trim to avoid TOCTOU.
- **Real provider tokens** instead of `chars/4`: `Response.InputTokens/OutputTokens` piped via `chat:usage` (live, emits both last-round and per-turn totals) and `chat:complete` (final). Frontend uses `lastInputTokens` for the context gauge and `totalInputTokens/totalOutputTokens` for per-turn cost in the assistant footer.
- **First-run edge cases**: `ProjectInfo.DirectoryOK` checks `os.Stat` of project dir on every `Info()`; red banner + send blocker on missing path. `GOKIN_CONFIG_DIR` env overrides the config root for tests.
- **Crash recovery**: per-session `{projectID}_{sessionID}.replay.jsonl` in `history/`. Appended for every user/tool_call/tool_result/thinking/assistant_text event; deleted on `chat:complete`. On session load, `GetRecoveryEvents` surfaces stale replay via a banner with [Recover into chat] / [Discard]. Recovery is informational — server-side history only holds the user turn from the interrupted round.
- **Smoke tests** (`internal/studio/*_test.go`): `compactHistory` (never splits function_call/function_response pairs, keeps latest exchanges), replay roundtrip, v1/v2 history format compatibility, `deriveSessionName`, `isDefaultSessionName`, `humanizeAPIError`, `summarizeRetryReason`, shared-memory write/read/TTL/type-filter/own-agent-filter.
- **Engine memory + plan wiring**: `memory.Store`, `memory.ProjectLearning`, `plan.Manager` created per project in `initMemoryAndPlan(reg)` and injected into the matching tools. `shared_memory` tool is wired to a process-wide `Studio.SharedMemory` for cross-project agent communication.

## What's Done (audit pass, iter 45+)

A sustained audit found and fixed the following — mostly silently-broken paths or races that wouldn't surface until real-world use:

- **saveConfig locking split** — the previous version deadlocked on every project mutation because a self-read-lock landed on a path already holding the write lock. Now `saveConfig` assumes the caller holds `s.mu`, and `saveConfigAsync` is the entry point for goroutines that don't.
- **history + replay cleanup on deletion** — `RemoveProject` / `DeleteChatSession` / `ClearHistory` now scrub the per-session history file AND the `*.replay.jsonl` buffer; previously only the legacy single-file history was removed, leaking state.
- **UpdateSettings invalidates client cache** — new API keys / URLs now take effect on the next send instead of after a restart.
- **Dispatch routing** — results route back to the originating session, not always to `default`. Dispatch self-target rejected; empty-task rejected; errors humanized.
- **/clear stops the active turn** before wiping history, so the agent goroutine can't append into a freshly-emptied session.
- **Tool panic recovery** via `safeToolExecute`, plus a top-level defer/recover in `p.SendMessage`, so a tool panic no longer crashes the whole Wails app.
- **Session-scope UI gating** — `ChatPanel` keyed all active-checks on the project-wide flag, which flip-flopped with every session start/stop. Now `sessionActive[chatKey]` drives each per-session gate (Send disabled, Stop button, elapsed timer, edit/rerun, isThinking).
- **Sidebar + StatusBar dots** now derive from `sessionActive` map instead of `project.active` (same flip-flop bug).
- **Path traversal hardening** — `isInsidePath` replaces the naive prefix match in `ListDirectory` / `ReadFileContent` so a sibling directory like `/home/user/foobar` is no longer accepted as being "inside" `/home/user/foo`.
- **Memory + ProjectLearning flush** on `Project.Stop` — the 2-second debounced save no longer loses last-minute writes on quick shutdown.
- **Background-task cleanup** — `Project.Stop` cancels running shells via `tasks.Manager.ListRunning()` so `run_in_background=true` processes don't outlive the project.
- **Esc overlay precedence** — overlays (FilePicker, CommandPalette, context menu) suppress the window-level Esc fallback, so closing a picker no longer accidentally stops the agent.
- **Recent-first sidebar + sessions** — projects and session tabs sort by `lastUsedAt` desc; a freshly-added project gets `lastUsedAt = now()` to land on top. Local bump via `bumpLastUsed` so the sort updates live without a full reload.
- **Empty session persistence** — `SaveHistoryWithName` writes even when history is empty if a name was set; `CreateChatSession` saves immediately, so a freshly-created tab survives a restart without a single message in it.
- **`ask_user` end-to-end** — registry, router-via-context, interactive frontend card (options + textarea + Ctrl+Enter/Esc). System prompt can now direct the agent to clarify ambiguous tasks.
- **Real provider tokens** in context gauge (last-round input) and per-turn cost footer (summed).
- **Inline diff viewer** for `edit` / `write`, colored `git_diff`, grouped grep output, syntax-highlighted `read`, clickable glob list.
- **Crash recovery banner** with summary of what was in flight ("3 tool calls (1 unfinished), partial reply"), Recover / Discard buttons.
- **IME composition guards** in every window-level keydown handler + the textarea Enter — no more swallowing a Cyrillic / CJK compose.
- **Emoji fallback fonts** in the base/mono stacks.
- **Dispatch preemptive warnings** — target cards with missing API key / missing directory are flagged; Send disabled for them.
- **FilePicker empty-state** distinguishes Loading / Empty / Failed with the real error message.
- **Open Settings button** in the missing-API-key warning.

## What's Done (Kimi + thinking pass, iter 60+)

Switching Kimi to its new coding-platform endpoint surfaced a cluster of
wire-format gotchas that had been silently masked by GLM's looser validator.
Each one now has a named regression test in `anthropic_convert_test.go`:

- **Kimi endpoint** -- `DefaultKimiBaseURL` = `https://api.kimi.com/coding`; sole model `kimi-for-coding` (Kimi-k2.6 under the hood, 262K ctx). Bearer auth only. Legacy model names (`kimi-k2.5`, `kimi-latest`, `moonshot-v1-*`) are auto-rewritten in `project.initClient` so saved configs keep working. `supportsPromptCaching` recognises kimi.com hostnames too.
- **tool_use_id propagation** -- `Project.SendMessage`'s tool-response builder now copies `fc.ID` onto `FunctionResponse.ID`. Previously the ID was dropped, `buildUserMessage` fell back to `fallback_<name>_<ordinal>`, and strict providers (Kimi) rejected with `tool_call_id is not found`.
- **Tool-result key** -- studio emits `FunctionResponse.Response = {"result": <output>}`; `buildUserMessage` / `convertHistoryWithResultsAndSystem` / `ollama.go` now accept `"result"` first, falling back to `"content"` / `"data"`. Before this fix every tool call reached the model as the literal string `"Operation completed"`, and the agent complained that tools "returned nothing".
- **Extended thinking (Kimi)** -- `Project.initClient` flips `EnableThinking=true, ThinkingBudget=4096` for the `kimi` provider. Anthropic.go forwards the `thinking` param and forces `temperature=1` automatically.
- **Thinking stream rendering** -- new `chat:thinking_delta` event, new `thinkingStream[chatKey]` buffer in chatStore, `appendThinkingStream` concatenates. ChatPanel renders a live thinking chip with pulsing Brain icon + word count above the streaming text, then `chat:thinking` (end-of-round) finalises it into a collapsed thinking message. Buffer is flushed on `addUserMessage` / `chat:complete` / `chat:error` so a dead turn doesn't leak reasoning into the next one.
- **Thinking round-trip** -- the SSE parser now captures `signature_delta`, emits a `genai.Part{Text, Thought:true, ThoughtSignature}` via `chunk.Parts` on `content_block_stop`. `recordResponse` preserves those parts in history; `buildAssistantMessage` emits `{"type":"thinking","thinking":...,"signature":...}` blocks back to the API. Legacy history (tool_use without a stored Thought) gets a stub thinking block injected when thinking is enabled, so pre-upgrade sessions don't start erroring 400 on first new send. `SaveHistoryWithName` skips Thought parts so reasoning doesn't pollute the plaintext transcript on reload.
- **Closeable default session** -- both the backend `DeleteChatSession` and the frontend close button no longer special-case `"default"`. The new guard is count-based (refuse to delete the last remaining session). `NewProject` only materialises a default if zero sessions were loaded from disk, so explicit deletions survive restart.
- **Paste from clipboard** -- Settings API-key inputs got a paste button using `wailsRuntime.ClipboardGetText`, because WebKitGTK blocks `navigator.clipboard.readText` on Linux.
- **Model chip on assistant messages** -- `ChatCompleteEvent` carries `Model` + `Provider`; the frontend writes them onto the last assistant message; MessageBubble renders a small muted chip in the footer so the user can tell which backend answered when switching providers mid-conversation.

## What's Done (iter 70+)

- **Per-project thinking toggle** -- `ProjectConfig` / `Project` / `ProjectInfo` now carry `ThinkingMode` (`""` = auto, `"enabled"`, `"disabled"`) and `ThinkingBudget` (int32, 0 = use provider default of 4096). `initClient` reads these instead of hardcoding Kimi. New `SetProjectThinking(id, mode, budget)` Wails binding. `ProviderSelect` dropdown adds a "Thinking" select + conditional budget input, wired through the store. Auto mode keeps backward-compat: Kimi still defaults to thinking on, all others to off.
- **Agent loop coverage** -- `agent_loop_test.go` with 11 tests covering: simple text, tool call + result (with mock `echoTool`), unknown tool, tool panic recovery (`safeToolExecute`), transient error retry (429 → retry event → success), non-retryable error (401 surfaces as `chat:error`), context cancellation, multi-turn history shape, `sendWithRetry` exhaustion, no-retry-on-non-retryable, context-cancel-stops-retry. Enabled by extracting a `testEmitter func` field on `Project` and refactoring `sendWithRetry` to accept a `notify` closure + `initialDelay` param. Both changes are production-transparent (nil emitter → Wails runtime; 0 delay → 2s default).
- **Thinking UX polish** -- (a) `ProviderSelect` resets `thinkingBudget` to 0 when mode switches away from "enabled", preventing stale budget leaking to the backend. (b) `.provider-select` popup gets `max-width: calc(100vw - 40px)` + `max-height: 70vh` + `overflow-y: auto` so it doesn't overflow narrow windows. (c) `SetProjectThinking` rejects negative budgets (frontend's `min="0"` is client-side only). (d) Chat header shows a purple `Brain · thinking` badge when thinking is active (mode=enabled, or Kimi on auto).
- **Slash command fix** -- `handleSend` now matches slash commands case-insensitively (`/CLEAR`, `/Clear`, etc.) instead of requiring exact lowercase.
- **Global default thinking mode** -- `Settings` struct gains `DefaultThinkingMode`/`DefaultThinkingBudget` (YAML + JSON serialized). `AddProject` applies them to new projects. Settings page gets a "Default Thinking" section (mode select + budget input when enabled). `App.tsx` loads both fields from `GetSettings`. Cascade to existing projects is intentionally omitted — per-project overrides are explicit choices.
- **Kimi context window fix** -- `contextWindowForProvider` now returns 262144 for `kimi` (Kimi-k2.6 has 262K context, not 128K). `ProjectInfo` gains a `ContextWindow int` field populated from this function on every `Info()` call. Frontend `projectStore.ts` `ProjectInfo` interface and `models.ts` `ProjectInfo` class both carry `contextWindow?: number`. `ChatPanel.tsx` uses `activeProject.contextWindow || 128000` instead of a hardcoded constant, so the context gauge and overflow warnings reflect the real window per model.
- **`shortenPath` macOS fix** -- `Sidebar.tsx` now handles both `/Users/<user>/...` (macOS) and `/home/<user>/...` (Linux) paths, collapsing them to `~/...`. Previously only Linux paths were shortened; macOS users saw full `/Users/ginkida/...` paths in the sidebar.
- **Clipboard write fallback** -- All `navigator.clipboard.writeText` calls in `ChatPanel.tsx` (message copy, code block copy, assistant message copy button) now go through a `copyToClipboard` helper that falls back to `ClipboardSetText` from the Wails runtime when the browser API is blocked. Symmetric with the existing `GetClipboardText` paste fallback in Settings.
- **File drop condition fix** -- `onDrop` handler in ChatPanel had a missing parentheses around the OR condition: `file && type.startsWith('text/') || file?.name.match(...)` evaluated as `(file && ...) || match`, meaning a null `file` variable could theoretically reach the FileReader. Fixed to `file && (type.startsWith('text/') || name.match(...))` which is correct and readable.
- **IME composition guards in rename inputs** -- `Sidebar.tsx` project rename input, add-project name/directory inputs, `App.tsx` tab rename input, and `CommandPalette.tsx` Enter handler all now check `e.nativeEvent.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229` before acting on Enter. Previously a Cyrillic/CJK compose-commit keystroke (Enter key) would accidentally trigger rename/submit instead of inserting the composed character.
- **FileBrowser session routing fix** -- `handleFileClick` in `FileBrowser.tsx` hardcoded `projectId + '_default'` as the chatKey and passed `''` to `SendMessage`, routing every file-to-chat action to the default session regardless of which tab the user was in. Fixed: `chatStore` gains `activeSession: Record<string, string>` + `setActiveSession(projectId, sessionId)`; `App.tsx` calls `setActiveSession` whenever `view` changes to a chat session; `FileBrowser` reads `chatStore.activeSession[projectId] || 'default'` and passes the resolved sessionId to both `addUserMessage` and `SendMessage`. Also dispatches `gokin:switch-tab` with the actual session ID (not the generic `'chat'` which resets to `'default'`) so the user lands on the right tab. `dropProject` in chatStore now also clears the `activeSession` entry on project removal.
- **FileBrowser stale-directory bug** -- `DirectoryNode` holds a `loaded` flag that was never reset when `activeProjectId` changed (React reconciles in-place, doesn't remount). Added `key={activeProjectId}` to force a full remount whenever the project switches, so the directory tree always shows the right project's files.
- **IME Escape guard gaps** -- `ChatPanel.tsx` window-level Escape handler and `DispatchModal.tsx` Escape handler both lacked `isComposing` checks, so pressing Escape to cancel CJK/Cyrillic composition would accidentally close the dispatch modal or stop the agent. Both now guard with `if (e.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229) return`. Same guard added to `FilePicker.tsx` React `onKey` handler (using `e.nativeEvent.isComposing`).
- **Ctrl+F search shortcut** -- The search button tooltip said "Ctrl+F" but no handler existed. Added a `Ctrl+F / Cmd+F` window-level keydown handler in `ChatPanel.tsx` (next to the existing Ctrl+P file-picker handler) that opens or closes the search panel, mirroring the header button behavior (close also clears the query). Intercepts the browser's find-in-page shortcut which is unused in a Wails desktop app.
- **ProviderSelect popup leak on project switch** -- `ProviderSelect` has its own `open` state that persisted when the user clicked a different project in the sidebar (the backdrop was not clicked). Added a `useEffect` that closes the popup whenever `activeProjectId` changes, so unsaved provider changes don't linger invisibly after switching away.
- **Light-mode code block contrast** -- `github-dark-dimmed` highlight.js theme produces light-gray token colours (`#adbac7`) that are near-invisible on the light theme's `#f7f7f9` background. Introduced a `--code-bg` CSS custom property (dark theme: `var(--bg-deepest)`; light theme: `#2d333b`, the hljs theme's own background) so code blocks stay dark in both themes and token colours remain readable. Updated `.md-content pre` and `.message-content pre` to use `--code-bg`.
- **IME guards in AskUserCard + message-edit** -- `AskUserCard` textarea `onKeyDown` and the inline message-edit textarea `onKeyDown` both lacked `e.nativeEvent.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229` guards. Pressing Escape or Ctrl+Enter during CJK/Cyrillic composition would cancel the card or submit the edit prematurely.
- **Ctrl+\` terminal shortcut** -- Added `Ctrl+\` / Cmd+\`` to the keyboard shortcut handler in `ChatPanel.tsx` alongside Ctrl+P and Ctrl+F; toggles the integrated terminal panel. Updated the terminal button tooltip to show the shortcut.
- **FileBrowser empty-state improvements** -- When `activeProject.directoryOK === false`, the file browser now shows a "Directory missing" message with the path instead of an empty tree. `DirectoryNode` now tracks `loadError` state and shows a "Failed to load directory" message when `ListDirectory` throws; also shows a spinner until the first load completes.
- **Ctrl+1/2/3 navigation** -- Ctrl+1 → Chat, Ctrl+2 → Files, Ctrl+3 → Settings. These were advertised in the Settings page shortcuts table but never implemented; sidebar "Settings" button showed wrong tooltip "Ctrl+5". Also updated CommandPalette action hints for "Open file browser" (Ctrl+2) and "Open settings" (Ctrl+3); updated Files and Settings tab title attributes with shortcut hints.
- **Shortcuts grid overhaul** -- Settings page shortcuts grid now lists all 14 actual shortcuts (Ctrl+1-3, Enter, Shift+Enter, Ctrl+L, Ctrl+T, Ctrl+K, Ctrl+P, Ctrl+F, Ctrl+`, Ctrl+PgUp/Dn, Escape, j/k) instead of only 4 incomplete entries.
- **Session navigation on project switch** -- `App.tsx` now navigates to `tabs[0].id` (the first loaded session, i.e. most-recently-used) instead of hardcoded `'default'` when switching projects. If 'default' was deleted, the app no longer shows a ghost session.
- **Dispatch Ctrl+Enter** -- `DispatchModal` textarea now accepts Ctrl+Enter to send the task. Added a "Ctrl+Enter to send" hint in the actions row.
- **System prompt Ctrl+Enter/Esc** -- System-prompt editor textarea now has `autoFocus`, Ctrl+Enter to save, Esc to cancel, and a hint text. IME guard added.
- **Context menu IME guard** -- Window-level keydown handler that closes the message right-click context menu on Escape now checks `e.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229` before acting.
- **Message action visibility on keyboard focus** -- `.msg-actions` and `.msg-duration` were only shown on `:hover`. Added `.message-row.focused` CSS rule so keyboard-navigated messages also reveal their action buttons.
- **Ctrl+1 smart navigation** -- `gokin:switch-tab` handler in `App.tsx` for `'chat'` now reads `chatStore.activeSession[pid]` to restore the user's last-visited session instead of hardcoding `'default'`. Same fix applied to the sidebar right-click "Open Chat" action. Ctrl+1 keyboard shortcut already had this fix; these are the remaining callers.
- **Search empty-state** -- When search is active and returns 0 matches, a "No messages match..." message now shows instead of the welcome screen appearing spuriously.
- **Auto-scroll includes ask_user / retry** -- The auto-scroll `useEffect` now depends on `askUserQ` and `retryStatus` so the question card and retry banner are always scrolled into view when they appear.
- **Sidebar ctx-menu Escape** -- The sidebar's project right-click context menu now closes on Escape (added `useEffect` + `keydown` listener guarded by `isComposing`).
- **j/k navigation uses filtered list** -- The vim-style message navigation handler used `messages` (unfiltered), so pressing j/k while search was active could focus hidden messages. Fixed to use `filteredMessages`. Also moved `filteredMessages` useMemo to before the navigation hooks so it's declared before use.
- **Message timestamps** -- `ChatMessage.timestamp` (non-zero for messages sent in the current session) is now shown as a subtle `HH:MM` time in the message footer, visible on hover or keyboard focus. History-loaded messages (timestamp = 0) show nothing. Full datetime on `title` attribute.

## What's Done (iter 80+)

- **Git branch refresh on completion** -- both `project:status` idle events (normal completion and panic recovery path) now include `gitBranch`. `useWailsEvents.ts` applies `updateProject(id, { gitBranch })` when the status goes idle so the sidebar shows the current branch after the agent runs `git checkout` / `git switch`.
- **Live retry countdown** -- the retry banner used a static `Math.round(delayMs / 1000)s` value. Added `retryCountdown` state with a 250ms interval effect that computes `Math.ceil((deadline - Date.now()) / 1000)`. Banner now shows "in 3s", "in 2s", ... "in 0s", "…" as the delay elapses.
- **ProviderSelect Escape handler** -- the provider/model popup had no keyboard close. Added a `useEffect` that registers a window `keydown` listener (with IME guard) while `open=true` and calls `handleCancel` via a `cancelRef` to avoid stale closure issues.
- **Session switch resets modals** -- switching sessions left `showDispatch` and `showMemory` open. Added both to the session-switch `useEffect` that already resets `showSearch`, `showSysPrompt`, etc.
- **Elapsed timer tied to chatKey** -- the elapsed-time interval effect depended only on `thisSessionActive`. If both sessions A and B were active and the user switched from A to B, `thisSessionActive` stayed `true` so the effect didn't re-run, leaving the counter at A's elapsed time. Added `chatKey` to the dependency array so the timer resets on any session switch.
- **Memory modal refresh button** -- the memory viewer loaded entries once on open. Added a `RotateCcw` refresh button in the modal header so users can reload entries after the agent has stored new memories mid-session.

## What's Done (iter 90+)

- **Tool pill completeness** -- `getToolPrimary` now covers `git_pr` (shows PR title), `task` (shows prompt), `todo` (shows action), `env` (shows variable name/prefix), `task_output`/`task_stop` (shows task ID), `diff`, `history_search`, `batch`, `memory`, `memorize`, `ask_agent`, `shared_memory`, `pin_context`, `update_scratchpad`. `getToolIcon` now uses `ListChecks` for todo/plan tools and `Bot` for task tool (in addition to ask_agent). Previously any tool not in the explicit list showed a generic `Zap` icon with no primary arg.
- **Welcome screen hidden when ask_user pending** -- `filteredMessages.length === 0 && !streamingText && !isThinking` condition that shows the welcome screen now also checks `!askUserQ`. Without this, an `ask_user` card in an otherwise-empty session would render on top of the welcome screen, showing both simultaneously.
- **Thinking parts leak in GetHistory / ExportChat** -- `GetHistory` (session switch) and `ExportChat` (markdown download) both iterated `c.Parts` and concatenated every `part.Text`, including `Thought`-flagged reasoning tokens. On session reload, raw model reasoning appeared as a regular assistant message. In exports it polluted the human-readable transcript. Fixed by adding `if part.Thought { continue }` guards in both functions, consistent with the guard already present in `SaveHistoryWithName`.
- **Project name validation parity** -- `AddProject` and `RenameProject` lacked the whitespace-trim and 60-char cap that `RenameChatSession` already applies. Both functions now `strings.TrimSpace` first, reject empty names, and truncate to 60 characters. A whitespace-only name no longer creates a project with an invisible title.
- **Modal reset on project switch** -- `ChatPanel`'s session-switch `useEffect` had only `currentSessionId` as a dep. Switching between projects whose first session happened to share the same ID (both "default") left `showDispatch`, `showMemory`, and `showSysPrompt` open from the previous project. Added `projectId` to the dependency array so a project change always resets all modal state.
- **Tool pill completeness (plan/agent tools)** -- `getToolPrimary` now handles `kill_shell` (shows `shell_id`), `ask_user` (shows question text), `enter_plan_mode` (shows plan title), `update_plan_progress` (shows `step N: action`), `exit_plan_mode` (shows reason), `get_plan_status` (no arg to show). Previously these all returned `null`, so their tool pills showed only the icon with no context.
- **Modal reset dep array bug fix** -- The `activeProjectId` was written as `projectId` (undefined) in the dep array added in iter 90+. Fixed to the correct variable name so the useEffect actually fires on project switch.
- **Context menu closes on scroll** -- Right-click message context menu was not closed when the user scrolled the chat list. It would float over wrong messages. Now attaches a `scroll` listener to `chatContainerRef` while the menu is open.
- **Add-project form Escape** -- Both inputs in the add-project inline form now handle Escape (with IME guard) to cancel and close the form, consistent with how other inline forms in the app work.
- **j/k focus cleared on search query change** -- When `searchQuery` changes, `focusedMsgId` is now cleared via a `useEffect`. Without this, a message focused in the full list would have `currentIdx === -1` in the filtered list, causing the next `j` press to jump to the first filtered result instead of navigating naturally.
- **getToolIcon completeness** -- `copy`/`move`/`delete`/`mkdir` now get `FolderTree`; `diff` gets `FileText`; `task_output`/`task_stop` get `TerminalSquare`; `ask_user` gets `MessageSquare`. Previously all of these showed the generic `Zap` fallback icon.
- **DeleteChatSession race fix** -- The "last remaining session" guard read `sessionCount` under RLock and then re-acquired the WLock for the actual delete. Two concurrent calls could both pass the guard with count 2, leaving a project with zero sessions. Fixed by holding WLock for the entire guard check + delete operation; `session.Stop()` is called after release so it doesn't need to re-acquire the lock.
- **File-drop size limit** -- `onDrop` in ChatPanel read the full file via `FileReader` before truncating at 100 KB. A large file (e.g. 50 MB lock file) would block the UI while buffering the entire content. Fixed by calling `file.slice(0, 100_000)` before `readAsText` so only 100 KB is ever loaded.
- **clearChat completeness** -- `clearChat` in `chatStore` was missing two fields: `drafts` (left a stale unsent draft visible after `/clear`) and `sessionActive` (left the "Generating" pill stuck if the stop-signal was delayed). Both are now reset. `doClear` in `ChatPanel` also calls `setInput('')` so the textarea itself empties immediately, not just on the next session switch.
- **`/search query` argument** -- Slash command handler now splits on the first space before matching, so `/search foo` opens the search panel with "foo" pre-populated. Previously any text after the command name was not parsed, so `/search foo` fell through to the agent. The `/search` autocomplete description updated to document the optional argument. All other slash commands continue to work as before (they receive and ignore the trailing argument).
- **Recovery banner flash on session switch** -- `GetRecoveryEvents` is async; old recovery state persisted until the new promise resolved, causing a brief flash of the previous session's recovery banner. Fixed by calling `setRecoveryEvents(null)` synchronously at the start of the effect before the async call.
- **Tab rename max-length** -- Session tab rename input now has `maxLength={60}` matching the backend 60-char cap. Without it, users could type unlimited characters and the backend would silently truncate on submit.
- **API key whitespace trimming in UpdateSettings** -- `UpdateSettings` now `strings.TrimSpace`s all four key/URL fields before saving. A common paste error (leading/trailing spaces from clipboard) would cause silent auth failures. Also clamps `DefaultThinkingBudget` to 0 if negative rather than persisting a bogus value.
- **maxLength parity on all rename/name inputs** -- Project name input in the add-project form and the sidebar project rename input now both have `maxLength={60}`, matching the backend cap and the session tab rename input that already had it.
- **Slash command keyboard navigation** -- ArrowDown/ArrowUp cycle through suggestions; Tab moves down; Enter applies the highlighted item; Escape clears the input. If there's exactly one match and nothing is highlighted, Enter still auto-applies it (existing behavior). A `slash-item-selected` CSS class highlights the active row.
- **Message timestamp shows date for old messages** -- `formatMsgTime` helper: today → `HH:MM`; yesterday → `Yesterday HH:MM`; older → `Apr 28` (short month + day). Previously all messages showed only time regardless of age, making week-old messages look recent.
- **Slash command autocomplete: Tab removed, Enter blocked** -- Tab was incorrectly treated as ArrowDown in the popup, preventing users from tabbing to the next focusable element while the popup was visible. Removed Tab from the navigation handler (ArrowDown/ArrowUp only). Additionally, pressing Enter while multiple matches were shown and nothing was selected would fall through and send the raw `/` text to the agent. Enter in that state now blocks the send without applying any command. The `slashMatchKey` dependency (joined cmd strings) replaces the `slashMatches.length` dep so selection resets correctly when the matching set changes at the same length (e.g., `/c` cycling between `/clear` and `/copy`).
- **Tab rename error badge** -- `commitRename` silently reverted the tab name on failure (optimistic update rolled back) with only a `console.error`. Users had no explanation for why the name flickered. Now shows a red `rename failed` badge on the tab for 3 seconds after a `RenameChatSession` error.
- **Thinking budget preserved across mode toggles** -- `SettingsPage` reset `defaultThinkingBudget` to 0 whenever the mode select changed away from `'enabled'`, so switching back to `'enabled'` always showed 0 instead of the user's previous value. Removed the conditional reset; the budget input is hidden when mode ≠ 'enabled' so there is no visual noise, and the value is preserved for when the user re-enables.
- **Memory delete error feedback** -- `DeleteMemoryEntry` failure was only logged to console. Added a `memError` state that shows a red error banner inside the memory modal for 3 seconds after a failed delete.
- **AskUserCard textarea maxLength** -- Textarea in the ask_user card had no length cap. Added `maxLength={4000}` to prevent accidental paste of very large text.
- **AskUserCard draft reset** -- `useEffect` that focuses the textarea on `question.questionID` change now also calls `setDraft(question.default || '')` and depends on `question.default`. If a new question arrives with the same ID but different default text, the draft now resets to the new default.
- **API key maxLength** -- API key inputs in SettingsPage now have `maxLength={500}` to prevent accidentally pasting a concatenated string (key + surrounding text).
- **Event handler null guard** -- `chatKey()` in `useWailsEvents.ts` now returns `null` when `data.projectID` is missing. All 9 event handlers (`chat:delta`, `chat:thinking_delta`, `chat:thinking`, `chat:text`, `chat:tool_call`, `chat:tool_result`, `chat:usage`, `chat:complete`, `chat:error`, `chat:ask_user`, `chat:retry`) guard with `if (!key) return`. `dispatch:complete` guards `data.from`. Previously a malformed event could silently update the `"undefined_default"` chatStore key, corrupting state.
- **FilePicker race fix** -- `toggle()` promise callbacks used the captured `next` snapshot (stale state) when updating `dirs`. Rapid double-clicks could make the second promise overwrite the first with an incorrect `expanded` value. Callbacks now spread `dd[path]` (current state at resolve time) instead of `next`.
- **FilePicker overflow notice** -- Search results were silently capped at 80. When more than 80 matches exist, a `"+N more — refine your query"` notice now appears below the list.
- **FileBrowser file-click debounce** -- `handleFileClick` had no guard against rapid clicks; multiple concurrent `ReadFileContent` calls could queue for the same file. Added `sending` state that blocks re-entry until the request completes, and adds an `opacity: 0.5; cursor: wait` visual cue.
- **DispatchModal hint** -- Keyboard hint "Ctrl+Enter" updated to "Ctrl/Cmd+Enter" since the handler already accepted both `ctrlKey` and `metaKey`.
- **ProviderSelect apply error + model guard** -- `handleApply` swallowed errors silently (only `console.error`). Now: (1) shows a red error banner inside the popup on any of the three API calls failing, keeping the popup open so the user can retry; (2) disables the Apply button when `model` is empty (can happen when a provider has no model list); (3) resets the error on Cancel/Escape.
- **System prompt save error** -- `SetProjectSystemPrompt` failure in the Ctrl+Enter path and the Save button handler was only logged to console. Now shows a `.sysprompt-error` banner inside the editor with the failure message. Error clears on Cancel, Esc, and session switch.
- **`GetSettings` data race** -- `GetSettings` returned `s.config` (the live pointer) while only holding a read lock. Wails serializes the return value AFTER the function returns (after the lock is released), so a concurrent `UpdateSettings` could race on `Settings` fields. Fixed by copying the struct under the lock (`c := *s.config`) and returning `&c`.
- **Ollama URL `maxLength`** -- Base URL input in SettingsPage now has `maxLength={300}`.
- **StatusBar thinking indicator** -- Status bar now shows a purple `Brain` icon after the model name when extended thinking is active (mode=enabled, or Kimi on auto). Matches the per-session indicator already shown in the chat header, but visible at all times.
- **`trimAndResend` clears focus ring** -- When editing/re-running a user message, `focusedMsgId` is now set to null so the vim navigation focus ring doesn't linger on a message that was trimmed from the list.
- **Session switch clears `focusedMsgId`** -- Added to the session-switch reset effect so navigation state from session A doesn't carry to session B.
- **System prompt error cleared on reopen** -- Both `/system` slash command and the menu button now call `setSysPromptError(null)` when opening the system prompt editor, so a previous failure banner doesn't persist into a fresh open.
- **FileReader.onerror handler** -- Added `reader.onerror` callback on the file-drop reader so failures (disk errors, permission denied) are logged rather than silently ignored.
- **Code block copy `.catch()`** -- `handleCodeCopy` in `CodeBlock` now handles clipboard failures; prevents an unhandled rejection from leaving the "Copied" state stuck. Context-menu copy also gets `.catch(() => {})` to suppress unhandled rejection warnings.
- **dropSession on session delete** -- Deleting a chat session previously called `clearChat` which left empty placeholder entries (`messages: []`, `streaming: ''`, etc.) for the deleted chatKey forever. Replaced with a new `dropSession(chatKey)` store action that removes the keys entirely, so the store doesn't accumulate stale entries for all previously-deleted sessions.
- **expandFileRefs logs failed reads** -- When `ReadFileContent` throws (path outside project, file missing, etc.), the failure was silently swallowed. Now logs `console.warn` with the path and error so it's visible in the Wails devtools console.

## What's Done (iter 100+)

- **AskUserCard async error handling** -- `AnswerQuestion` failure was silently swallowed: the card always dismissed even if the backend call failed, leaving the agent goroutine waiting forever. Redesigned `AskUserCard` to be async-aware: `onAnswer` prop is now `Promise<void>`; the card shows a `submitting` spinner, keeps itself open on error, and renders a `.askuser-error` banner. Option buttons and the textarea are disabled while submitting. The `onAnswer` handler in ChatPanel no longer wraps in try/catch — errors propagate to the card. Only `setAskUser(chatKey, null)` (dismiss) is called on success.
- **`expandFileRefs` per-file cap** -- `@path` file expansions had no per-file byte limit beyond what the backend's `ReadFileContent` returns (up to 100 KB each). With 10 files that could send 1 MB to the LLM. Added `EXPAND_FILE_MAX_BYTES = 50_000` constant; content is sliced at 50 KB with a truncation note.
- **Streaming ReactMarkdown performance** -- All inline `[rehypeHighlight]` arrays replaced with two module-level constants: `MD_REHYPE_PLUGINS` (used by finalized messages) and `MD_STREAM_PLUGINS = []` (used during streaming). Previously a new array was created on every render, forcing ReactMarkdown to re-run the rehype pipeline even when content barely changed. During streaming, syntax highlighting is also intentionally skipped (incomplete code blocks, fires every delta); the finalized message renders with full highlighting.
- **Tab close navigation** -- Closing a tab jumped to `remaining[remaining.length - 1]` (the last tab). Now picks the adjacent left neighbor (or first if closing the leftmost tab), matching browser-tab UX. Uses `closedIdx = prev.findIndex(...)` and `nextIdx = Math.max(0, closedIdx - 1)`.
- **Sidebar rename error feedback** -- `handleRename` kept `setEditingName(null)` in `finally`, so the rename input silently disappeared even on failure. Now: success closes the input; error keeps it open and shows a `.sidebar-inline-error` banner so the user can see why the rename failed and try again.
- **Sidebar remove error feedback** -- `handleRemove` silently swallowed the error (only `console.error`), the confirm dialog closed as if delete succeeded. Now shows a `.sidebar-inline-error` banner inside `project-info` for 4 seconds, and only calls `setConfirmDelete(null)` on success. Cancel button also clears the error.
- **ProviderSelect thinking budget preserved** -- `ProviderSelect` had the same "reset budget to 0 when switching away from enabled" bug that was already fixed in `SettingsPage`. Removed the conditional `setThinkingBudget(0)` reset; the budget input is hidden when mode ≠ 'enabled', so value is preserved for re-enable.
- **DispatchModal UX polish** -- added `autoFocus` to the task textarea (immediate typing), `maxLength={10000}` to prevent accidentally pasting huge content, and auto-close after 1.8 seconds on success (previously required manual cancel after the success message).
- **Session-switch reset covers FilePicker** -- `showFilePicker` was not reset when switching sessions, so an open file picker would linger across session changes. Added `setShowFilePicker(false)` to the session-switch `useEffect`.
- **`projectLang` stale on project switch** -- language detection for the welcome screen didn't clear `projectLang` before the async `ListDirectory` call. If you switched from a Go project to a Python project, the "Go project" label flashed briefly until the new call resolved. Fixed by calling `setProjectLang(null)` before the async fetch.
- **`trimAndResend` / `rerun` rollback on failure** -- both functions optimistically trimmed the message list, then called `EditUserMessage`. If the backend rejected the edit (agent already running, project gone, network error), the UI was left visibly trimmed but the backend never actually re-ran. Now both functions snapshot `beforeMsgs` and restore on error + finalize an `Error: ...` assistant message so the user knows what happened.
- **`StopGeneration` / `ClearHistory` error feedback** -- previously silent (only `console.error`). User pressed Stop and agent kept running with no indication; user pressed Clear and history might still exist. Now both surface the failure as an `Error: ...` assistant message in the chat.
- **Tab close/create error badges** -- `handleNewChat` and `handleCloseChat` in App.tsx silently swallowed errors. Added `newChatError` and `closeError` transient states that render `tab-badge.err` (matching the existing `renameError` pattern). The "+" tab gets a "failed" badge on `CreateChatSession` failure; the closed tab gets "close failed" if `DeleteChatSession` rejects.
- **Ctrl+PgUp/PgDn from non-chat view** -- `cycleHandler` returned `current` (no-op) when the view was Files or Settings since `findIndex` returned -1. Now jumps into chat at the first session instead of silently doing nothing.
- **Auto-select next project on remove** -- `removeProject` in `projectStore` set `activeProjectId` to `null` when the active project was removed, dumping the user into a blank "No project selected" state. Now picks the most-recently-used remaining project (sorted by `lastUsedAt`); falls back to `null` only when no projects remain.
- **Atomic config + history writes** -- `Save()` (config.yaml) and `SaveHistoryWithName` (per-session JSON) used plain `os.WriteFile`, which is not atomic. A mid-write crash or full disk could leave a half-written / corrupt file, losing the project list, API keys, or chat history. Added an `atomicWriteFile` helper in `history.go` that writes to `path+".tmp"` then renames (atomic on POSIX); both call sites now use it.
- **FileBrowser SendMessage error feedback** -- `handleFileClick` in `FileBrowser.tsx` had `SendMessage(...).catch(() => {})`, silently dropping send failures. The user saw `[File: path]` appear in the chat but never got a response. Now surfaces as `Error: failed to send file — <reason>` so the user knows nothing was actually sent.
- **FileBrowser ReadFileContent error feedback** -- `handleFileClick`'s `ReadFileContent` failure (file moved, permission denied, too large) was only `console.error`'d. The click did nothing visible and the user wondered if the picker was broken. Now switches to the active session and adds an error message so the failure is visible in the chat.
- **FileBrowser sub-directory load error** -- `FileNode.handleToggle` swallowed `ListDirectory` failures (`.catch(() => setLoaded(true))`), leaving the user staring at an empty expanded folder with no explanation. Added `loadError` state and an inline `file-node-error` row showing "Failed to load: <error>" beneath the failed directory.
- **Chat input maxLength=100000** -- the main message textarea had no character cap. A multi-megabyte paste (`.lock` file, full PDF text) would freeze the UI while React re-rendered the textarea on every keystroke, and the eventual SendMessage would shovel the whole blob through the Wails bridge. Capped at 100K chars; normal messages are <1KB, even pasted file content fits comfortably.
- **System prompt + message-edit textarea caps** -- both the per-project system prompt editor and the in-chat message edit textarea were uncapped. Added `maxLength={20000}` (system prompt) and `maxLength={100000}` (message edit) to mirror the chat input cap.
- **Search input maxLength caps** -- ChatPanel `chat-search-input` (500), FilePicker `fp-input` (500), CommandPalette `palette-input` (200) all had no character cap. A runaway paste into any of them would force-recompute filtered results on every keystroke. Capped at sane limits — search queries are typically <50 chars.
- **BrowseDirectory error feedback** -- the Browse... button in the add-project form silently swallowed errors (`console.error` only). On Linux without a native file picker daemon (e.g. some minimal WMs), the dialog would fail to open and the user would think the button was broken. Now writes the error to the existing `form-error` banner so the user can see why and either type the path manually or fix their environment.
- **Settings clipboard paste error** -- when both GetClipboardText (Wails native) and navigator.clipboard.readText (browser API) failed, the Paste button did nothing with no feedback. Added `pasteErrors: Record<string, string | null>` state in SettingsPage; shows a `.api-key-paste-error` inline message "Clipboard unavailable — paste manually (Ctrl+V)" for 4 seconds below the relevant key field. Error clears on next paste attempt.
- **Language detection completeness** -- `suggestionsForLang` now also detects Swift (`Package.swift`), Dart (`pubspec.yaml`), Zig (`build.zig`), C# (any `.csproj`/`.sln`/`global.json`), and C/C++ (`CMakeLists.txt`, `configure.ac`, `meson.build`). Each gets language-appropriate welcome screen suggestions (e.g. `swift build`, `dart analyze`, `dotnet build`, `cmake`).
- **Session view validation on Ctrl+1 and tab-switch** -- `setView(lastSession || 'default')` in the keyboard handler and `gokin:switch-tab` handler would set the view to a deleted session ID. Both now use `setSessions` functional form to validate the restored session exists in the current tab list; if not, they fall back to the first available session.
- **Async effect stale-response race** -- switching projects or sessions rapidly could overwrite the new project/session's data with a stale async response from the previous one. Three effects now carry a `let cancelled = false` guard with a cleanup `return () => { cancelled = true }`: (1) `GetHistory` in ChatPanel (session history load), (2) `GetRecoveryEvents` in ChatPanel (recovery banner), (3) `ListDirectory` in ChatPanel (language detection), (4) `ListChatSessions` in App.tsx (session tab list). Any response that arrives after the effect was torn down is silently dropped.
- **Session naming after deletion** -- `CreateChatSession` used `len(sessions) + 1` as the "Chat N" number, so deleting a session (e.g. "Chat 2" of "Chat 1, 2, 3") would produce a new "Chat 3" that duplicated the existing one. Now scans all session names for the highest existing "Chat N" number and uses `max + 1`, guaranteeing unique display names even after deletions.
- **ExportChat failure surfaced** -- `/export` command and the "Export as Markdown" menu item silently swallowed errors (only `console.error`). User clicked Export, nothing downloaded, no explanation. Now calls `finalizeAssistant` with an `Error:` message so the failure is visible in chat.
- **Memory modal load error** -- `openMemoryViewer()` and the Refresh button in the memory modal both set `memoryEntries = []` on failure, making the modal show "Nothing remembered yet." (misleading) instead of the real error. Both paths now also call `setMemError(...)` so the red error banner appears above the entry list.
- **Memory discoverability** -- "Project memory" added to the `...` chat header menu (alongside System prompt / Dispatch / Export). "View project memory" action added to the Ctrl+K command palette (group: Actions, hint: `/memory`). Event `gokin:open-memory` dispatched by both so `ChatPanel` can open the modal from any caller.
- **`gokin:open-memory` stale closure fix** -- the event listener registered in `ChatPanel` used `useEffect([], [])` (empty deps), capturing the initial `openMemoryViewer` which closed over the initial `activeProjectId`. Switching projects after the component mounted meant the memory modal always loaded the first project's entries. Fixed by moving the listener effect to after `openMemoryViewer` is declared, using a `useRef` updated on every render (`openMemoryViewerRef.current = openMemoryViewer`) so the handler always calls the current version. Also: the ref was originally placed before the function declaration, causing a TypeScript "used before declaration" error (TS2448). Moved ref creation+assignment to immediately after the function definition.
- **Sidebar context menu viewport clamping** -- Sidebar's right-click project context menu was positioned at raw mouse coordinates with no edge detection. Near the bottom of the screen, the menu would overflow below the viewport. Changed from a simple `style={{ top: ctxMenu.y, left: ctxMenu.x }}` to an IIFE that clamps: `cy = Math.min(ctxMenu.y, window.innerHeight - MENU_H - 8)` and `cx = Math.min(ctxMenu.x, window.innerWidth - MENU_W - 8)`. ChatPanel's message context menu already had this fix; now Sidebar matches.
- **`handleNewChat` rapid-click guard** -- clicking "+" or pressing Ctrl+T rapidly could trigger multiple concurrent `CreateChatSession` calls, creating duplicate session tabs. Added `creatingChatRef` (a `useRef<boolean>`, not state) so the guard works correctly across stale closures in the keyboard shortcut handler. The callback is now stable (`useCallback` with `[]` deps); `creatingChatRef.current` is set/cleared around the async call.
- **ProviderSelect Escape stops agent** -- pressing Escape while the ProviderSelect popup was open and an agent was running caused `handleStop()` to fire in `ChatPanel`'s window-level Escape handler. The handler already deferred to `.fp-backdrop` and `.palette-backdrop` but missed `.provider-select-backdrop` and `.dispatch-backdrop`. Added both to the bail-out selector: `document.querySelector('.fp-backdrop, .palette-backdrop, .provider-select-backdrop, .dispatch-backdrop')`.
- **AskUserCard Escape cancels question** -- pressing Escape while the `ask_user` card was visible but keyboard focus was NOT in its textarea (e.g., focus on the Cancel/Answer buttons) caused `handleStop()` to fire instead of dismissing the card. Fixed by reading `useChatStore.getState().askUser[chatKey]` from the store inside the Escape handler (so it's never stale) and calling `CancelQuestion` + `setAskUser(null)` before the "stop agent" fallback. Also added `chatKey` to the effect's dep array.
- **Session-switch resets header menu** -- the `...` header menu (`showMenu`) was not in the session-switch reset effect, so cycling sessions with Ctrl+PgUp/PgDn could leave the menu open. Added `setShowMenu(false)` to the reset effect.
- **Context window refreshed after provider switch** -- `updateProject` after a successful `handleApply` in `ProviderSelect` only updated locally-known fields. Switching from GLM (128K) to Kimi (262K) left the context gauge at the old window size until the next startup or settings save. Now calls `GetProject(projectId)` after apply and updates `contextWindow` from the backend response.
- **FileBrowser subdirectory load error retry** -- `FileNode.handleToggle` set `loaded=true` even on error, so collapsing and re-expanding a failed directory never retried the `ListDirectory` fetch — the error was stuck permanently until the top-level refresh button was used. Changed the condition to `!expanded && (!loaded || loadError)` so any expand after a failure triggers a fresh fetch, clearing the error state first. Closes the usability gap for transient network/permission errors on subdirectories.
- **`saveConfigAsync` data race** -- the function read `s.config.Settings` AFTER releasing `s.mu.RLock()`, racing with concurrent `UpdateSettings` calls that write `s.config.Settings = cfg.Settings` under the write lock. Struct assignment is not atomic for multi-field structs in Go. Fixed by reading `settings := s.config.Settings` inside the `RLock`/`RUnlock` block before the unlock.
- **FilePicker subdirectory error retry + display** -- `toggle()` in `FilePicker.tsx` had the same "never retries on error" pattern as FileBrowser. After a `ListDirectory` failure: `loaded` was set to `true`, `!cur.loaded` guard blocked forever, and the error was silently dropped (not stored in `DirState.error`). Fixed: condition changed to `willExpand && (!cur.loaded || cur.error)` to retry on re-expand after failure; `error` field stored in state on `.catch(err)`; `renderNode` now shows "Failed to load: <error>" instead of an empty subtree. Retry resets `loaded: false, error: undefined` so the in-flight period shows nothing (consistent with first-expand behavior).
- **`GetSession` nil guard in `SendMessage` and `ClearHistory`** -- `GetSession` returns `nil` when the requested session ID is not found AND the default session doesn't exist (e.g., "default" was explicitly deleted). `SendMessage` would immediately panic on `session.mu.Lock()` (caught by the top-level recover with a confusing "Internal error" message), and `ClearHistory` would do the same. Added explicit `if session == nil` guards in both: `SendMessage` emits a `chat:error` with "session not found: <id>" and returns cleanly; `ClearHistory` returns an error to the caller.
- **Memory modal Escape key fix** -- the memory modal's backdrop used `dispatch-backdrop` (same class as DispatchModal). The window-level Escape handler bails out early when `.dispatch-backdrop` is in the DOM (to defer to DispatchModal's own Escape handler), so pressing Escape while the memory modal was open never reached the `if (showMemory)` close branch. Fixed by giving the memory modal a unique `memory-backdrop` class with matching CSS in `App.css`.
- **`ExportChat` data race on `p.Name`** -- `ExportChat` read `p.Name` in `sb.WriteString` after releasing `p.mu.RUnlock()`. `RenameProject` writes `p.Name` under `p.mu.Lock()`, so these could race. Fixed by capturing `pName := p.Name` inside the `p.mu.RLock()` block before unlock and using `pName` in the string builder.
- **`dispatchInternal` data races on project names** -- `dispatchInternal` read `from.Name` and `to.Name` (in the `emitError` closure, in the prompt string, and in the final event emit) without holding any lock. `RenameProject` writes project `Name` under `p.mu.Lock()`. Fixed by capturing both names under their respective `p.mu.RLock()` at the start of the function and using the captured values throughout.
- **Duplicate tool_result blocks in agent loop** -- `project.go` appended function responses to `session.history` BEFORE calling `SendFunctionResponse`, so `historySnapshot` (last element = the responses) AND `funcResponses` both contained them. Every provider (`convertHistoryWithResultsAndSystem` for Anthropic/GLM/MiniMax/Kimi, GeminiClient, OllamaClient) appended the `results` param as a new user turn after converting history, resulting in each tool result appearing twice. With strict providers (Kimi) this caused 400 errors; with lenient ones (GLM) it was silently masked. Fix: snapshot history BEFORE appending, then append after `sendWithRetry` succeeds but before `recordResponse` (preserving causal order). `agent_loop_test.go` now asserts no `FunctionResponse` parts in the `history` arg to `SendFunctionResponse`.
- **Chained tool-call rounds broken** -- when the model responded to tool results with more FunctionCalls, those FCs fell through to the next outer-loop iteration which called `SendMessageWithHistory` with a spurious "Continue." message. `sanitizeToolPairs` silently removed the unresolved FunctionCall parts from history, forcing the model to redo them — an extra round-trip per chained call. With strict APIs (Kimi) it would also 400 on the orphaned call IDs. Fix: moved tool execution + `SendFunctionResponse` into an inner `for toolRound < 40` loop so chained rounds stay within one `SendMessageWithHistory` turn. The outer `for turns < 50` loop always breaks after the inner loop exits; the outer label allows `break outer` from the inner loop on error. New test `TestAgentLoop_ChainedToolCalls` verifies exactly 3 LLM calls (not 4) and correct history shape for two consecutive tool rounds.
- **Coverage additions** -- five new tests filling previously unverified paths: `TestAgentLoop_ParallelFCsInOneRound` (multiple FCs in a single model response are all executed and their results sent in one `SendFunctionResponse` call; callCount=2, not 3); `TestGetHistory_FiltersThinkingParts` (thinking/reasoning parts never appear as assistant text on session reload); `TestCreateChatSession_NamingAfterDeletion` + `TestCreateChatSession_NamingGapFill` (new sessions always get `max(existingN)+1` to avoid collisions after deletions, not `len(sessions)+1`); `TestDeleteChatSession_CannotDeleteLast` (backend rejects deleting the final session so a project is never left with zero chats); `TestEditUserMessage_TrimsHistoryAndRequeues` (edit trims history back to the target user turn and re-queues a fresh agent run via the studio WaitGroup path).
- **Project management tests** (`project_test.go`) -- 8 new tests covering `AddProject` (empty/whitespace name rejected; non-existent directory rejected; duplicate directory rejected; name over 60 chars truncated; leading/trailing whitespace trimmed), `RenameProject` (unknown ID, empty name, valid rename with `GetProject` verification), `RemoveProject` (unknown ID, valid removal verified via `GetProject`/`ListProjects`), `SetProjectSystemPrompt` (unknown ID, stored prompt verified via `GetProject`), `SetProjectThinking` (invalid mode "always", negative budget, unknown ID, enabled+budget stored, disabled).
- **FileBrowser empty-file click** -- `handleFileClick` in `FileBrowser.tsx` checked `if (content)` which is `false` for empty files (empty string is falsy in JS). Clicking an empty file did nothing with no feedback. Fixed by checking `content !== null && content !== undefined`; empty files now display as `(empty file)` in the fenced block so the agent knows the file was intentionally read.
- **Settings + session operation tests** (`settings_test.go`) -- 6 new tests: `UpdateSettings` trims API key whitespace (`TestUpdateSettings_TrimsKeyWhitespace`), clamps negative `DefaultThinkingBudget` to 0 (`TestUpdateSettings_ClampsNegativeBudget`), persists all fields through `GetSettings` round-trip (`TestUpdateSettings_PersistsSettings`), nullifies cached clients so new API keys take effect immediately without restart (`TestUpdateSettings_InvalidatesClientCache`); `RenameChatSession` validation (empty name, unknown project/session, valid rename verified via `ListChatSessions`) and `ClearHistory` (unknown project rejected, valid clear wipes in-memory history).
- **Message edit + dispatch validation tests** (`message_edit_test.go`) -- 3 new tests: `TestEditUserMessage_Validation` (table-driven; empty text, whitespace text, negative index, unknown project, out-of-range index each produce appropriate errors); `TestEditUserMessage_ActiveSessionRejected` (session with `active=true` returns "agent is running" error); `TestDispatch_Validation` (self-dispatch, empty task, whitespace task, unknown source, unknown target each rejected).
- **File-ops tests** (`file_ops_test.go`) -- 10 new tests covering `ListDirectory` and `ReadFileContent`: unknown project, path traversal rejection (`../../etc/passwd` → "outside project directory"), directory listing (dirs-first alphabetical, hidden/noise excluded), subdirectory listing with relative `Path` field, file read, empty-file read returns `""` (not an error), 100KB truncation with `[truncated at 100KB]` notice, missing file error.
- **Session query tests** (`session_query_test.go`) -- 3 new tests: `TestGetHistory_UnknownProject` (error for missing project); `TestGetHistory_FiltersFunctionCallTurns` (model-FunctionCall and user-FunctionResponse turns absent from output — only user-text and model-text survive); `TestListChatSessions_SortOrder` (sessions sorted lastUsedAt desc, never-used at bottom, "default" last among ties).
- **Recovery + defaults tests** (`recovery_test.go`) -- 5 new tests: `TestGetRecoveryEvents_NoFile` (nil, nil when no replay exists); `TestGetRecoveryEvents_ReturnsPendingEvents` (incomplete turn returned); `TestGetRecoveryEvents_CompleteMarkerDropsSilently` (replay with "complete" marker discarded, nil returned, file removed); `TestDiscardRecoveryEvents_RemovesFile` (discard makes next call return nil); `TestApplyDefaultToProjects` (all projects get default provider/model from settings).
- **Project provider/params tests** (`project_test.go` additions) -- 2 new tests: `TestSetProjectProvider` (unknown project errors, provider+model stored, client cache nil'd); `TestSetProjectModelParams` (unknown project errors, temperature+maxTokens stored).
- **Disk cleanup tests** (`cleanup_test.go`) -- 5 new tests: `TestRemoveProject_CleansUpHistoryAndReplay` (history file + replay log removed when project deleted); `TestDeleteChatSession_CleansUpHistoryAndReplay` (per-session files removed on session delete, other sessions' files untouched); `TestClearHistory_DiscardsReplay` (replay log removed on clear so recovery banner can't resurrect a cleared turn); `TestStopGeneration_UnknownProject` (error for missing project); `TestStopGeneration_StopsSpecificSession` (session cancel function is called via context). Studio test suite now has 88 tests total.

## What's Done (iter 110+)

Continued test suite expansion targeting previously-unexercised public API paths and helper functions:

- **Memory CRUD tests** (`memory_ops_test.go`) -- 7 new tests: `ListProjectMemory` with unknown project (error), nil store (returns empty slice not error), and live store with an added entry (entry appears with correct Content+Type fields); `DeleteMemoryEntry` with unknown project (error), nil store (error "memory not initialised"), non-existent entry ID (error), and full success lifecycle (add entry → list → delete → list returns empty). Covers two public API methods with 0% prior coverage.
- **StopGeneration all-sessions path** (`cleanup_test.go` addition) -- `TestStopGeneration_EmptySessionIDStopsAll`: creates a project with two sessions, wires cancel functions onto both, calls `StopGeneration(pid, "")` (empty sessionID → `p.Stop()` path), verifies both contexts are cancelled. Completes coverage of the empty-sessionID branch.
- **Session pruning tests** (`session_prune_test.go`) -- 5 new tests for `pruneAbandonedEmptySessions`: single-session guard (zero-session invariant never violated); session with history preserved; explicitly-renamed session preserved; all-auto-named guard (at least one survives even when all are candidates); active sessions never pruned. Previously zero test coverage on startup-time prune logic.
- **Context window tests** (`context_window_test.go`) -- `TestContextWindowForProvider` with 10 cases: kimi → 262144; glm-5.x and glm-4 → 128000; minimax default → 128000; unknown provider → 128000; ollama+llama3.1 → 128000 (profile); ollama+qwen2.5-coder → 32768 (profile); ollama+unknown → 4096 (GetModelProfile unknown fallback). Documents the unreachable 8192 fallback in the ollama branch.
- **DirectoryOK lifecycle test** (`project_test.go` addition) -- `TestGetProject_DirectoryOK`: verifies `ProjectInfo.DirectoryOK` is `true` for an existing directory and `false` after `os.Remove`. This field drives the "directory missing" red banner in the frontend — previously untested.
- **ListProjects sort tests** (`list_projects_test.go`) -- `TestListProjects_SortOrder` (most-recently-used first, lastUsedAt=0 last); `TestListProjects_NameTiebreaker` (equal non-zero timestamps sort alphabetically by name). Covers the 60%-uncovered sort comparator branches.
- **EditLastUserMessage delegation test** (`list_projects_test.go` addition) -- `TestEditLastUserMessage`: uses `newTestProject` (mock client + testEmitter) to run the full trim+re-send cycle without Wails runtime; verifies the 4-entry history shape after edit, and the unknown-project error path. Brings `EditLastUserMessage` from 0% to 100% coverage.

Studio test suite: **106 tests** total (up from 88), all passing.

## What's Done (iter 120+)

Continued test suite expansion targeting previously-unexercised branches in session/replay/sort logic:

- **`ChatSession.Info()` coverage** (`session_info_test.go`) -- 6 new tests bringing `Info()` from 55.6% to 100%: empty history (Messages=0, metadata fields); text-only history (count=2); tool-only history (FunctionCall+FunctionResponse with no text parts, Messages=0 — the inner-loop "no break" path); mixed history (text turns counted, tool turns skipped); Active flag propagation; LastUsedAt propagation. The inner for-loop body was never reached by existing tests since all test sessions had empty history.
- **`ReplayLogger` nil-receiver + post-close guards** (`replay_test.go` additions) -- 3 new tests: `TestReplayLogger_NilReceiverSafe` (nil `*ReplayLogger` can call Append/Complete/Close without panic — the `if r == nil { return }` guards); `TestReplayLogger_AppendAfterClose` (events written after `Close()` are silently dropped — the `if r.closed { return }` guard); `TestReplayLogger_AppendAfterComplete` (Complete removes the file and marks closed, so a subsequent Append cannot recreate it). `Complete` and `Close` go from 83%/80% to 100%.
- **`GetSession` fallback paths** (`session_query_test.go` additions) -- `TestGetSession_EmptyIDDefaultsToDefault` (passing `""` resolves to `"default"`); `TestGetSession_FallbackToDefault` (unknown session ID falls back to default rather than returning nil). Both take `GetSession` from 71.4% to 100%.
- **`ListChatSessions` edge cases** (`session_query_test.go` additions) -- `TestListChatSessions_UnknownProject` (error returned for missing project — the function's early-return path); `TestListChatSessions_ZeroLastUsedTiebreaker` (two sessions with lastUsedAt=0, neither "default": older CreatedAt sorts first); `TestListChatSessions_DefaultLastAmongZeros` (when other sessions also have 0 lastUsedAt, "default" always sorts last — the `result[j].ID == "default" → return true` comparator branch). Takes `ListChatSessions` from 70.8% to 95.8%.
- **`contentSize` nil-part + non-string arg branches** (`misc_helpers_test.go` additions) -- `TestContentSize_NilPartSkipped` (nil entry in Parts slice skipped, does not panic); `TestContentSize_NonStringArgs` (integer FunctionCall arg uses 32-byte estimate; non-string FunctionResponse value uses 64-byte estimate). Takes `contentSize` from 86.4% to 100%.

Studio test suite: **127 tests** total (up from 106), all passing. Coverage: 70.0% (up from 68.9%).

## What's Done (iter 130+)

Continued test suite expansion targeting history loading, NewProject migration paths, and edge cases:

- **History load edge cases** (`history_load_test.go`) -- 10 new tests covering previously-untested paths: `TestLoadHistoryRaw_EmptyFile` (empty/zero-byte file treated as no history, not a parse error); `TestLoadHistoryRaw_WhitespaceOnlyFile` (whitespace-only file also treated as empty); `TestLoadHistoryRaw_CorruptLegacyArray` + `TestLoadHistoryRaw_CorruptVersionedObject` (malformed JSON files return descriptive error). `loadHistoryRaw` goes from 76.5% → 94.1%; `atomicWriteFile` 75% → 100% (via `TestAtomicWriteFile_DirectoryMissing`).
- **`NewProject` disk-loading paths** -- `TestNewProject_LoadsSessionsFromDisk` (session files on disk are loaded on startup — exercises the disk-loading loop body previously at 0%); `TestNewProject_FallbackSessionName` (sessions without a stored name get "Chat XXXX" fallback from first 4 chars of session ID); `TestNewProject_LegacyMigrationCaseB` (Case B: non-default sessions loaded + legacy single-file history → legacy adopted as "default" then deleted). `NewProject` goes from 70.6% → 94.1%.
- **`project.Info()` active-session branch** (`history_load_test.go`) -- `TestProjectInfo_ActiveSession`: sets `session.active = true`, calls `GetProject`, verifies `ProjectInfo.Active = true`. The `anyActive = true` branch was never reached by any prior test. `project.Info` goes from 86.7% → 100%.
- **`DeleteChatSession` unknown-session path** -- `TestDeleteChatSession_UnknownSession`: creates 2 sessions so the last-session guard doesn't fire, then tries to delete a nonexistent session ID. Covers the `!exists` error return (was at 85% → 95%).
- **`GetRecoveryEvents` empty sessionID** -- `TestGetRecoveryEvents_EmptySessionID`: writes a replay log for "default" and calls `GetRecoveryEvents(pid, "")` — the empty-ID → default fallback path (function goes 86.7% → 93.3%).
- **`SaveHistoryWithName` thinking-parts stripping** -- `TestSaveHistoryWithName_ThinkingPartsSkipped`: history with `Thought: true` parts saved and reloaded — thinking text must not appear in the persisted transcript. `SaveHistoryWithName` goes 85% → 90%.
- **`compactHistory` edge cases** (`compact_test.go` additions) -- `TestCompactHistory_EmptyHistory` (nil and zero-length input return unchanged, no panic); `TestCompactHistory_SingleExchangeNotTrimmed` (history exceeding budget but with only 1 exchange returns unchanged — "cannot trim safely" guard). Both bring `compactHistory` from 93.5% → 100%.
- **`RenameProject` 60-char truncation** -- `TestRenameProject_TruncatesLongName`: 80-char name capped to 60 chars, matching `AddProject` cap. `RenameProject` goes from 93.3% → 100%.
- **`GetHistory` empty sessionID** -- `TestGetHistory_EmptySessionIDDefaultsToDefault`: calling with `""` routes to "default" session (was 92.9% → 96.4%).

Studio test suite: **144 tests** total (up from 127), all passing. Coverage: 71.4% (up from 70.0%).

## What's Done (iter 140+)

Continued test suite expansion targeting previously-uncovered branches in `EditUserMessage`, `RemoveProject`, `ClearHistory`, `ExportChat`, `LoadReplay`, `sendWithRetry`, and several small validation paths:

- **`EditUserMessage` 100%** -- three new tests filling all remaining gaps: `TestEditUserMessage_EmptySessionIDDefaultsToDefault` (empty sessionID → "default"); `TestEditUserMessage_UnknownSessionInKnownProject` (error for unknown session in a valid project); `TestEditUserMessage_SkipsNoTextUserTurns` (FunctionResponse-only user turn is skipped by the `!hasText` guard, so the next text user turn is found). `EditUserMessage` goes 93.9% → 100%.

## What's Done (iter 190+)

- **`coordinate` tool graceful fallback** -- when no `coordinatorFactory` is wired (the current studio default), `Execute` fell through with `"coordinator not configured"` which gave agents no actionable guidance. Fixed by parsing args first and calling `executeSimple` when factory is nil. `executeSimple` returns a structured "Tasks to execute:" list with a note to use the available tools sequentially. Change: moved arg parsing before factory nil-check in `coordinate.go:Execute`; also fixed the guidance text which incorrectly referenced the unwired `task` tool — now says "Execute each task sequentially using the tools available to you (bash, read, write, edit, git, web, etc.)".

## What's Done (iter 200+)

- **README accuracy pass** -- updated provider descriptions (Kimi now correctly shows api.kimi.com/coding endpoint + 262K ctx), added per-project thinking toggle to feature list, added missing keyboard shortcuts (Ctrl+1/2/3, Ctrl+\`, j/k), updated test coverage blurb (237 tests, 92%), added missing tools (`coordinate`, `ask_user`), fixed architecture section (FileBrowser, ProviderSelect, StatusBar description, agent/ framework note).
- **`CancelQuestion` fix** -- was calling `resolve(id, "(user cancelled the question)")` which sent a confusing pseudo-answer string to the agent handler. The agent would receive `"User answered: (user cancelled the question)"` and potentially act on it as if it were a real response. Fixed by adding `askUserRegistry.cancel(id)` which closes the channel without sending; the blocked handler now receives `ok=false` and returns `fmt.Errorf("user dismissed the question")`, causing the tool to return an error result. Agents cleanly see the question was dismissed rather than getting a fake answer. Added `TestCancelRegistry_ClosesChannel` test; updated `TestStudioCancelQuestion` to verify the new close-channel behavior. Studio test suite: **238 tests**.

## What's Done (iter 210+)

- **`pin_context` and `history_search` actually wired** -- these tools appeared in the system prompt and `ToolSetMemory` declarations but were silently broken: `DefaultRegistry` (what studio uses) never included them — only the unconnected `LazyRegistry` did. The agent would see them in the system prompt, try to use them, and get `"not supported by this agent"`. Fixed three layers: (1) Added `memorize`, `pin_context`, and `history_search` to `DefaultRegistry` so they're offered to the model in tool declarations; (2) Wired `pin_context` in `initMemoryAndPlan` — `SetWorkDir(p.Directory)` + `SetUpdater(func updating p.pinnedContext)` + `LoadPersistedPin()` to restore from disk; (3) In `SendMessage`, if `p.pinnedContext != ""`, call `c.SetSystemInstruction(base + pinnedContext)` before the agent loop so pinned notes survive history compaction; (4) Wired `history_search` via a per-session context key (`tools.HistoryGetterCtxKey`) injected into `toolCtx` in the tool-call loop — each session supplies its own live getter closure so concurrent sessions can't race on the shared tool's stored field.
- **4 new tests** covering the new code paths: `TestInitMemoryAndPlan_PinContextWired` (updater wires correctly, `Execute` sets `p.pinnedContext`); `TestSendMessage_PinnedContextApplied` (pinned content reaches `SetSystemInstruction`); `TestSendMessage_NoPinNoSystemInstructionUpdate` (no extra call when pin is empty); `TestAgentLoop_HistorySearchViaContext` (tool finds pre-seeded history entry via context-injected getter, nil stored getter). Studio test suite: **242 tests**, **92.3% coverage**.
